# Skills · F · part 66

812 entries, fishers-exact-contingency-testing to five-questions. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **fishers-exact-contingency-testing**: Use when discrete, mutually exclusive counts form a 2x2 table needing an exact P-value — e.g. insertion-carrying vs wild-type reads split by intronic/exonic class at a SCISSOR-flagged junction, or co-altered gene counts between cohorts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fishers-exact-contingency-testing))
- **fishers-exact-enrichment-testing**: Use when comparing binary trait counts (e.g., inverted-Alu-positive vs. Alu-negative BSJs, tissue-specific isoform vs. gene) against a matched control set, and a 2x2 contingency table with scipy.stats.fisher_exact is appropriate. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fishers-exact-enrichment-testing))
- **fishers-exact-enrichment-testing**: Use when testing whether a lead variant set (e.g. sQTLs at FDR &lt; 0.05) is enriched in discrete annotation categories relative to matched non-sQTLs or intron sets, and you need odds ratios with Fisher's exact p-values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fishers-exact-enrichment-testing--e8a3d461f6f13fbb))
- **fishers-exact-enrichment-testing**: Use when a single-cell dataset has categorical clone assignments (e.g., mitochondrial clones from vireoSNP at probability &gt;0.8) and independent cell labels (tumor vs non-tumor, CNV subclones) and you need to test enrichment of one clone in one label. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fishers-exact-enrichment-testing--945518df1c660909))
- **fishers-exact-enrichment-testing**: Use when comparing a set of cell-type co-localization pairs detected on reconstructed or predicted spatial coordinates against a reference set of supported co-localizations, to quantify concordance with a p-value. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fishers-exact-enrichment-testing--6704c02b327a91d1))
- **fishers-exact-enrichment-testing**: Use when two sample groups yield sets of candidate regions (e.g., predicted dsRNA regions per sample) and you need to test whether the shared vs group-specific overlap fractions differ significantly between groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fishers-exact-enrichment-testing--7d0e79bbdab75c19))
- **fishers-exact-hypergeometric-enrichment-testing**: Use when a list of differentially abundant metabolites (metabolites of interest) must be tested against a pathway set and background set to obtain per-pathway enrichment p-values and identify significantly enriched pathways. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fishers-exact-hypergeometric-enrichment-testing))
- **fishers-exact-ora-testing**: Use when input is a spatial/single-cell metabolomics matrix with METASPACE MS1 annotations (e.g. FDR &lt;= 0.1, HMDB) and you need ORA while accounting for ions matching multiple isomeric/isobaric candidates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fishers-exact-ora-testing))
- **fishers-exact-pathway-enrichment**: Use when you have a set of significant metabolites (e.g. absolute fold change &gt;= 1 and adjusted p &lt; 0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/fishers-exact-pathway-enrichment))
- **fishers-exact-proportion-testing**: Use when two discrete cell samples have been clustered (e.g. k=5 ChromSCape clusters) and you need a significance test that the proportion of cells falling in a target cluster differs between the samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/fishers-exact-proportion-testing))
- **fishers-exact-receptor-association-testing**: Use when you have predicted molecular subtype labels (e.g., from a DeepCC model via get_DeepCC_label) plus cohort clinical metadata with ER/PR/HER2 receptor status and survival outcomes, and need to demonstrate clinical relevance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/fishers-exact-receptor-association-testing))
- **fishers-exact-test-variant-quality-control**: Use when filtering candidate SNV lists derived from single-molecule long reads (PacBio, Oxford Nanopore) whose allele observations are significantly over-represented on one read strand prior to haplotype assembly or genotyping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fishers-exact-test-variant-quality-control))
- **fishers-exact-testing-of-splicing-order-ratios**: Use when you have categorical upfi/dofi (or Fupfi) counts from two sequencing modalities or conditions and need to formally test agreement of splicing-order proportions, e.g. validating a long-read bias correction against a short-read reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/fishers-exact-testing-of-splicing-order-ratios))
